pymusicxml.spanners
*******************

Module containing all spanners (i.e. notations that span a time-range
in the score, such as slurs, brackets, hairpins, etc.)

-[ Classes ]-

+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| "ChangePed | Pedal change in the middle of a sustain pedal spanner.                                     |
| al"([label |                                                                                            |
| , sign,    |                                                                                            |
| line,      |                                                                                            |
| placement, |                                                                                            |
| ...])      |                                                                                            |
+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| "StartBrac | Start of a bracket spanner.                                                                |
| ket"([labe |                                                                                            |
| l,         |                                                                                            |
| line_type, |                                                                                            |
| line_end,  |                                                                                            |
| ...])      |                                                                                            |
+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| "StartDash | Start of a dashed spanner (e.g. used by a dashed "cresc." or "dim." marking).              |
| es"([label |                                                                                            |
| , dash_len |                                                                                            |
| gth, ...]) |                                                                                            |
+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| "StartHair | Notation to attach to a note that starts a hairpin                                         |
| pin"(hairp |                                                                                            |
| in_type[,  |                                                                                            |
| label,     |                                                                                            |
| spread,    |                                                                                            |
| ...])      |                                                                                            |
+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| "StartPeda | Start of a sustain pedal spanner.                                                          |
| l"([label, |                                                                                            |
| sign,      |                                                                                            |
| line,      |                                                                                            |
| placement, |                                                                                            |
| ...])      |                                                                                            |
+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| "StartSlur | Notation to attach to a note that starts a slur                                            |
| "([label]) |                                                                                            |
+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| "StartTril | Starts a trill spanner with a wavy line.                                                   |
| l"([label, |                                                                                            |
| placement, |                                                                                            |
| accidental |                                                                                            |
| ])         |                                                                                            |
+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| "StopBrack | End of a bracket spanner.                                                                  |
| et"([label |                                                                                            |
| ,          |                                                                                            |
| line_end,  |                                                                                            |
| end_length |                                                                                            |
| , ...])    |                                                                                            |

    * "StopTrill.wrap_as_score()"


======================================================================
=== FILE: pymusicxml.txt
======================================================================

pymusicxml package
******************

A simple utility for exporting MusicXML files that represents musical
objects more hierarchically.

While it is of course possible to create MusicXML files in Python
directly via ElementTree, the format is awkwardly non-hierarchical.
For instance, there are no separate objects for chords and tuplets;
rather they exist only as special annotations to the note objects
themselves. In pymusicxml, chords are represented similarly to notes
but with multiple pitches, and tuplets are treated as containers for
notes, chords, and rests.

At the moment, this library is intended as a tool for composition,
making it easier to construct and export MusicXML scores in Python. In
the future, the ability to parse existing MusicXML scores may be
added.

-[ Modules: ]-

+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| "direction | Module containing all non-spanner subclasses of the "Direction" type.                      |
| s"         |                                                                                            |
+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| "enums"    | Module containing relevant Enums used throughout pymusicxml.                               |
+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| "notations | Module containing all non-spanner notations, such as glisses, bowings, fermatas, etc.      |
| "          |                                                                                            |
+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| "score_com | Module containing all of the classes representing the hierarchy of a musical score.        |
| ponents"   |                                                                                            |
+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
| "spanners" | Module containing all spanners (i.e. notations that span a time-range in the score, such   |
|            | as slurs, brackets, hairpins, etc.).                                                       |
+------------+--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
